Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the elliptic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 861. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 509 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 861 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the elliptic structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 509. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
